Task 4.4 – Grant Strategy Assistance 
 
• 
HDR will support the City of Cheyenne in identifying potential available grant funding 
opportunities for construction. 
o 
This includes a workshop with 2 HDR staff and the City to discuss what types of funding 
they are interested in. 
o 
The resulting document(s) from the Grant Strategy Assistance task will be a technical

deadlines to submit.

•

o 
This includes HDR technical experts in hazardous materials handling to review and

Task 4.6 – BNSF Railroad Coordination 
• 
HDR will prepare for and participate in a scoping and routing meeting with the City and the BNSF 
Railroad.   
o 
This includes a potential storm interceptor crossing of the existing railroad tracks at Reed 
Avenue as well as a conceptual discussion about routing south along Reed Avenue. 
 
This assumes that any agreements or reimbursements required by the railroad 
will be initiated by the City OR can be combined with existing efforts on the Reed 
Avenue Rail Corridor Project. 
o 
HDR will prepare two exhibits for the meeting.  This includes meeting attendance by 
three HDR staff.  Meeting may be held virtually or in person. 
• 
It is assumed that no railroad insurance or right of entry agreements will be prepared or provided 
by HDR.

2 1 3 L I N D E N S T R E E T , S U I T E 3 0 0 | F O R T C O L L I N S , C O L O R A D O 8 0 5 2 4 | 9 7 0 . 4 4 9 . 4 1 0 0 | W W W . L O G A N S I M P S O N . C O M 
 
May 16, 2025 
 
Todd Mattson 
PE 
HDR Inc. 
 
Dear Todd,  
We are excited to submit this proposal for the Cheyenne 18th St. Streetscape project. 
Task 1. Project Startup and Pre-design 
We will begin with a focused Kick-off Meeting to confirm the scope, define deliverables, establish roles and 
responsibilities, and set clear communication standards. This will ensure alignment between all stakeholders from the 
outset. Additionally, we will conduct an in-depth Work Session and Site Visit to review the project boundary and collect 
field data and develop character base maps. 
Task 2.  Concept/ Schematic Design 
Task 2 will begin the design process with our team developing 3 concepts with key areas shown in plan and 3D views with 
building massing for context. We will have an order of magnitude opinion of probable costs for concept evaluation and 
discussion with key stakeholders for the first engagement meeting. Completion of schematic design will include a 
preferred plan developed in AutoCAD. 
Task 3.  Design Development 
Design development will include CAD drawings including all areas within the limit of work to a 60% complete 
construction document level.  Specifications will be included in outline form, with an opinion of probable cost. 
Task 4.  Construction Documentation 90% and 100%  
Construction documents will include both 90% coordination documents, and final 100% Construction documents. Both 
deliverable milestones will include specifications and opinion of probable costs. 
 
Sincerely, 
 
 
Jana McKenzie, Principal/Contract Manager 
Steve Sigler, Senior Landscape Architect/Project Manager 
 
E:  jmckenzie@logansimpson.com  
 
E: ssigler@logansimpson.com

Memorandum of Understanding between the City of Cheyenne and Cheyenne Frontier Days, Inc. 
Page 1 of 5 
 
 
CONTRACT NO. _________ 
 
MEMORANDUM OF UNDERSTANDING 
BETWEEN THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
AND CHEYENNE FRONTIER DAYS, INC. 
 
This Memorandum of Understanding (“MOU”) is made and executed this _______ day of 
_________________, 2025, by and between the City of Cheyenne, a municipality duly organized 
and existing under the laws of the State of Wyoming (“City”), whose address is 2101 O’Neil 
Avenue, Cheyenne, Wyoming 82001, and Cheyenne Frontier Days, Inc., a Wyoming corporation 
(“CFD”), whose address is P.O. Box 2477, Cheyenne, Wyoming 82003, in which the parties agree 
as follows: 
 
RECITALS 
 
1. 
The City owns land and buildings near Frontier Park, which have been used for parking, 
camping, and other activities by CFD. Specifically, the City owns: 
 
a. 
Land around Lake Absarraca, which is described as that area bordered by Kennedy 
Road on the south, Interstate 25 on the west, the Governor’s Mansion property on 
the northwest corner, and the Airport Golf Course on the north, northeast and east; 
and 
 
b. 
The “E lot” parking area described as north of Lions Park Drive, south of the 
Municipal Aquatics Center, and east of Carey Avenue. 
 
2. 
The City and CFD have, in the past, entered into agreements regarding the use of the 
City-owned parcels and structures. This MOU is substantially similar to earlier agreements. 
 
3. 
During the 2025 Cheyenne Frontier Days celebration, CFD proposes to: 
 
a. 
Provide overnight camping at Lake Absarraca, from the Sunday preceding the 
Cheyenne Frontier Days celebration through the Monday after the celebration ends; 
and 
 
b. 
Provide public parking at the “E lot.” There will be no overnight parking in the “E 
lot.” This additional parking for automobiles in the confines of Lions Park is to 
facilitate access to the Cheyenne Frontier Days Old West Museum and relieve 
parking pressure in the Avenues neighborhood. 
 
 
2:40 pm, Jun 04, 2025

 
Memorandum of Understanding between the City of Cheyenne and Cheyenne Frontier Days, Inc. 
Page 2 of 5 
 
WHEREFORE, in consideration of the mutual promises by the City and CFD made each 
to the other, the parties agree as follows: 
 
1. 
CFD will: 
 
a. 
Maintain control in the areas covered by this MOU; 
 
b. 
Provide portable toilet facilities for the areas covered by this MOU; 
 
c. 
Mark and designate camping and parking slots to maximize use of the space; 
 
d. 
Provide car parking and revenue collectors; 
 
e. 
Allow for public access to Lions Park along Lions Park Drive during the 
celebration; 
 
f. 
Police and patrol the campground on a 24-hour basis; 
 
g. 
Encourage RV units to utilize the CFD provided sanitation vendors; 
 
h. 
Provide an adequate area and access for day fishermen at Lake Absarraca; 
 
i. 
Keep all grounds clean from litter and empty trash receptacles daily, or as needed. 
Coordinate with the City Sanitation Department for placement of additional 
dumpsters to be placed at Lake Absarraca; 
 
j. 
Provide proof of insurance as follows: CFD agrees to file with the City, Certificates 
of Insurance verifying each type and amount of insurance coverage listed below. 
Certificates will be submitted to the City prior to commencement of performance 
under this MOU. 
 
Type of Coverage 
 
 
 
Minimum Limits 
 
 
Commercial Automobile Liability 
 
$1,000,000 combined single limit 
$5,000,000 excess or umbrella 
 
 
Commercial General Liability 
 
$1,000,000 per occurrence 
$2,000,000 aggregate 
 
 
Excess Liability Coverage 
 
 
$5,000,000 per occurrence 
$5,000,000 aggregate 
 
 
Workers’ Compensation 
 
 
Statutory 
 

 
Memorandum of Understanding between the City of Cheyenne and Cheyenne Frontier Days, Inc.

Employer’s Liability Stop Gap 
 
$500,000 each accident 
 
(if afforded by voluntary Workers’ 
 
$500,000 each disease-policy limit 
 
Compensation)

It is understood and agreed that these policies are primary and not contributory. It 
shall be an affirmative obligation upon CFD to immediately notify in writing the 
city risk manager, city clerk, and city attorney of any fact, circumstances, or 
occurrence that has resulted in or may result in the cancellation or substantive 
change of any insurance coverage required by this MOU, and failure to do so shall 
be construed to be a breach of this MOU. 
 
The City of Cheyenne shall be named as an additional insured on CFD’s insurance 
policies, except Workers’ Compensation, and CFD shall provide, upon request, a 
copy of an endorsement providing this coverage. 
 
In addition, CFD shall provide the City with copies of insurance policies and/or 
policy endorsements listing the City of Cheyenne as an additional insured. The 
City’s failure to request or review such policies, endorsements, or certificates shall 
not affect the City’s right or CFD’s obligation hereunder. 
 
The City has the right to reject a certificate of insurance if CFD’s insurance 
company is widely regarded in the insurance industry as financially unstable. Any 
insurance company providing coverage under this MOU shall have a minimum 
A.M. Best rating of A- (Excellent); 
 
k. 
Provide daily clean up and final clean-up of all areas covered by this MOU; 
 
l. 
Not allow permit parking; all parking will be fully paid; 
 
m. 
Restore the ground surface in “E lot” to its condition prior to the 2025 Cheyenne 
Frontier Days celebration; and 
 
n. 
Provide an accounting of revenue and expenses for all revenue producing areas 
following the end of the 2025 Cheyenne Frontier Days celebration. Such 
accounting shall be submitted to the City Clerk no later than November 1, 2025. 
 
2. 
The City will: 
 
a. 
Provide adequate dumpsters for the areas covered by this MOU and empty daily; 
 
b. 
Provide daily maintenance of associated restroom facilities including all supplies, 
excluding the facilities at Lake Absarraca, which will be maintained by CFD; and 
 

 
Memorandum of Understanding between the City of Cheyenne and Cheyenne Frontier Days, Inc. 
Page 4 of 5 
 
c. 
Assist with T-post installation for fencing of the areas, as decided by the parties 
prior to the Monday preceding the Cheyenne Frontier Days celebration. 
 
3. 
The parties agree that the first fourteen thousand dollars ($14,000.00) to be generated from 
the parking lot and camping rentals will be remitted to CFD as part of the City's 
contribution to the annual 4th of July fireworks celebration. 
 
4. 
It is further agreed that the remainder of the parking lot and camping net revenue, after the 
contribution to the 4th of July fireworks celebration and payment of expenses associated 
with collection of parking and camping revenue, shall be divided equally between the 
parties and CFD shall deliver to the City, the City’s revenue portion with the accounting 
of revenue and expenses pursuant to Section 1(n) of this MOU. The remainder of the City’s 
portion of these funds will be deposited into the City’s Parks Project Fund. 
 
5. 
The City agrees to rent the E-Lot and Lake Absarraca Campground to CFD for one dollar 
($1.00). 
 
6. 
This MOU shall commence June 1, 2025, and terminate August 31, 2025, unless sooner 
terminated by either party upon thirty (30) days written notice. This shall not affect CFD’s 
obligations under Section 1(n) of this MOU. 
 
7. 
CFD agrees to comply with all applicable federal, state and local laws, rules and 
regulations. 
 
8. 
The City expressly invokes its governmental immunity for any claim arising out of this 
MOU pursuant to the Wyoming Governmental Claims Act, Wyo. Stat. ' 1-39-101, et seq. 
 
9. 
CFD agrees to indemnify, hold harmless and defend the City from and against any and all 
liabilities, claims, penalties, forfeitures, and suits, and the cost and expenses incident 
thereto including reasonable attorney’s fees, which may hereafter arise as a result of death 
or bodily injury to any person, destruction, or damage to any property, contamination of or 
adverse effects on the environment, or any violation of governmental laws, regulations, or 
orders to the extent caused by (1) CFD’s breach of any term or provision of this MOU, or 
(2) any negligent or wrongful act, error, or omission by CFD, or its employees or 
subcontractors in the performance of this MOU. CFD acknowledges that it may incur a 
financial obligation to the City pursuant to the terms of this paragraph. 
 
10. 
This MOU contains the entire understanding of the parties. There are no other terms or 
conditions, written or oral, concerning or controlling this matter. 
 
11. 
The parties agree that this MOU shall not become effective and enforceable until it has 
been approved by the parties’ respective governing bodies. 
 
 

 
Memorandum of Understanding between the City of Cheyenne and Cheyenne Frontier Days, Inc. 
Page 5 of 5
